- **inspect-skill**: Statically inspect an agent skill, plugin, or marketplace directory with Casefile before it is installed. Use when the user asks whether a downloaded or third-party skill, plugin, or marketplace is safe to install, wants it audited or scanned first, or wants to check whether a reviewed artifact has changed.
